		Max_Char_Len = 40
		);
	CREATE TABLE WORK.SOLAR_NEGATIVAR_SCPC AS
		SELECT DISTINCT A.*
			FROM CONNECTION TO PERM
				(
select distinct *

from bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_corp_cobranca.batimentonegativacao_solarhist_fato
where dt_processamento = (
  select max(dt_processamento)
  from bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_corp_cobranca.batimentonegativacao_solarhist_fato
)
and ds_decisao_scpc = 'NEGATIVAR'
) AS A
	;
	DISCONNECT FROM PERM
	;
QUIT;



proc sort data=SOLAR_REABILITAR_SCPC;
	by sk_batimento_solar dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=SOLAR_REABILITAR_SCPC nodupkey out=SOLAR_REABILITAR_SCPC_OK;
	by sk_batimento_solar dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=SOLAR_REABILITAR_SERASA;
	by sk_batimento_solar dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=SOLAR_REABILITAR_SERASA nodupkey out=SOLAR_REABILITAR_SERASA_OK;
	by sk_batimento_solar dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=CARTAO_REABILITAR_SERASA;
	by sk_batimento_cartoes dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=CARTAO_REABILITAR_SERASA nodupkey out=CARTAO_REABILITAR_SERASA_OK;
	by sk_batimento_cartoes dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=CARTAO_REABILITAR_SCPC;
	by sk_batimento_cartoes dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=CARTAO_REABILITAR_SCPC nodupkey out=CARTAO_REABILITAR_SCPC_OK;
	by sk_batimento_cartoes dt_processamento;
run;

data CARTAO_BAT_TOTAL_2;
	set CARTAO_BAT_TOTAL;

	format ds_acao_serasa_n $32.;
	format ds_acao_scpc_n $32.;

	if fl_acordo=1 and ds_acao_serasa='NEGATIVAR' then ds_acao_serasa_n='OK';
	else ds_acao_serasa_n=ds_acao_serasa;

	if fl_acordo=1 and ds_acao_scpc='NEGATIVAR' then ds_acao_scpc_n='OK';
	else ds_acao_scpc_n=ds_acao_scpc;

run;

proc sort data=CARTAO_BAT_TOTAL_2;
	by sk_batimento_cartoes dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=CARTAO_BAT_TOTAL_2 nodupkey out=CARTAO_BAT_TOTALOK;
	by sk_batimento_cartoes dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=SOLAR_BAT_TOTAL;
	by sk_batimento_solar dt_processamento;
run;

proc sort data=SOLAR_BAT_TOTAL nodupkey out=SOLAR_BAT_TOTALOK;
	by sk_batimento_solar dt_processamento;
run;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE RODAS_BAT_SERASA_ATR AS
SELECT
	A.*
	,b.*,
case when A.cd_contrato = B.NUCONTRATO then 1 else 0 end as FLAG_CONSTA
FROM WORK.RODAS_BAT_SERASA AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.CARTEIRA_RODAS_EMP	AS B ON A.cd_contrato = B.NUCONTRATO
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE RODAS_BAT_SCPC_ATR AS
SELECT
	A.*
	,b.*,
case when A.cd_contrato = B.NUCONTRATO then 1 else 0 end as FLAG_CONSTA
FROM WORK.RODAS_BAT_SCPC AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.CARTEIRA_RODAS_EMP	AS B ON A.cd_contrato = B.NUCONTRATO
;QUIT;

data RODAS_BAT_SCPC_OK;
	set RODAS_BAT_SCPC_ATR;

	format tp_acao_correcao_n $35.;

	if tp_status_base='REABILITADO' then tp_acao_correcao_n='OK';
	else if tp_acao_correcao='BAIXAR' and QTATRASO > 45 then tp_acao_correcao_n='OK';
	else tp_acao_correcao_n=tp_acao_correcao;

run;

data RODAS_BAT_SERASA_OK;
	set RODAS_BAT_SERASA_ATR;
	format tp_acao_correcao_n $35.;

	if tp_status_base='REABILITADO' then tp_acao_correcao_n='OK';
	else if tp_acao_correcao='BAIXAR' and QTATRASO > 30 then tp_acao_correcao_n='OK';
	else tp_acao_correcao_n=tp_acao_correcao;

run;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE SOLAR_BAT_TOTALOK_3 AS
SELECT
	A.*
	,b.DECISAO as DECISAO_MIS,
case when A.cd_contrato = put(B.NUCONTRATO,z14.)  AND A.sq_contrato_financeiro = B.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO then 1 else 0 end as flag_consta
FROM WORK.SOLAR_BAT_TOTALOK_2 AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.BASE_CONFERENCIA_SOLAR	AS B ON A.cd_contrato = put(B.NUCONTRATO,z14.)  AND A.sq_contrato_financeiro = B.SQCONTRATOFINANCEIRO
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CONTRATOS_RODAS_CESSAO_2 AS
SELECT
	A.*,
case when put(a.NUCONTRATO,z14.) = B.cd_contrato   then 'SIM' else 'NAO' end as RESTRICAO_SERASA,
case when put(a.NUCONTRATO,z14.) = c.cd_contrato  then 'SIM' else 'NAO' end as RESTRICAO_SCPC

FROM WORK.CONTRATOS_RODAS_CESSAO_NOV_25 AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.RODAS_BAT_SERASA_3	AS B ON put(a.NUCONTRATO,z14.) = B.cd_contrato
LEFT JOIN WORK.RODAS_BAT_SCPC_3	AS c ON put(a.NUCONTRATO,z14.) = c.cd_contrato

;QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 094 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
DATA WORK.CONTRATOS_RODAS_CESSAO_NOV_25;
    LENGTH
        NUCONTRATO         8 ;
    FORMAT
        NUCONTRATO       BEST14. ;
    INFORMAT
        NUCONTRATO       BEST14. ;
    INFILE '/saswork/SAS_work2C2F00149BD3_srv-sasprd03n1/#LN00012'
        LRECL=14
        ENCODING="LATIN1"
        TERMSTR=CRLF
        DLM='7F'x
        MISSOVER
        DSD ;
    INPUT
        NUCONTRATO       : ?? BEST14. ;
RUN;

CREATE TABLE BOLETOS_PAGOS_HO_202510 AS
SELECT
	A.*
FROM
	CONNECTION TO PERM (
SELECT DISTINCT *

FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dbcontencioso.tbnegociacaoparcela
WHERE datetime(dtpagamento) BETWEEN DATETIME("2025-10-01") AND
DATETIME_ADD("2025-10-01", INTERVAL 1 MONTH) and dtpagamento IS NOT NULL
;

)A;

QUIT;
RUN;

LIBNAME adamor  '/riscosbv/cobranca/adamor';

proc sql;
create table BASE_AUX_CADASTRO_SYNAPSE as select

	a.cd_contrato_cyber as CONTRATO_CYBER,
	a.cd_contrato as CONTRATO_2,

	cd_cpf_cnpj as CPFCNPJ_2,
	cd_cpf_cnpj AS CPFCNPJ,

	nm_cliente as NOME_CLIENTE_2,
	vr_risco_atraso as RISCO_1,
	vr_risco_contrato as RISCO_2,
	vr_risco_agencia as RISCO_3,
	dt_venc_parcela as DT_OCORRENCIA format ddmmyy10.,
	c.DtNascimento as DT_NASCIMENTO,

	DT_ACORDO,
	b.uf as uf_cyber,
	b.TP_PESSOA,
	input(b.CEP,best8.) as CEP,
	b.ENDER as ENDERECO,
	b.NuEndereco,
	b.Complemento,
	b.BAIRRO,
	b.CIDADE,
	b.UF,
	b.MUNICIPIO,
	b.DtCadastro,
	b.RnkDedup,
	b.RnkGr

from CARTAO_REABILITAR_SERASA_OK  a left join tbpessoaendereco b
on compress(a.cd_cpf_cnpj)=compress(b.IdPessoa)

left join tbpessoa as c
on compress(a.cd_cpf_cnpj)=compress(c.IdPessoa)

;
quit;

proc sort data=BASE_AUX_CADASTRO_SYNAPSE;
	by CONTRATO_2 descending DtCadastro;
run;

proc sort data=BASE_AUX_CADASTRO_SYNAPSE nodupkey out= BASE_AUX_CADASTRO_SDUP;
	by CONTRATO_2;
run;

data BASE_CADASTRO_REAB;
set BASE_AUX_CADASTRO_SDUP (where=(CPFCNPJ not in ("",'.') ));
	N=_N_;
	NUSQREGISTRO=N+1;

	FORMAT NUSQREGISTRO2 $7.;
	NUSQREGISTRO2=PUT(NUSQREGISTRO,Z7.);

drop
n;
run;



data aux_04;
set BASE_CADASTRO_REAB;

format NOME_CLIENTE $52.;


NOME_CLIENTE=TRIM(NOME_CLIENTE_2);

run;

data aux_04;
set aux_04;

	IF TP_PESSOA IN ('PF',"") THEN TP_PESSOA= 'F';
	ELSE
	IF TP_PESSOA = 'PJ' THEN TP_PESSOA = 'J';
	ELSE
	TP_PESSOA = TP_PESSOA;

RUN;

data  base_aux_cadastro (keep= CONTRATO_2 CONTRATO_CYBER CPFCNPJ TP_PESSOA NOME_CLIENTE  RISCO_1 RISCO_2 RISCO_3
								DT_OCORRENCIA DT_NASCIMENTO ENDER COMPLEMENTO BAIRRO CIDADE MUNICIPIO UF
								CEP DT_COMPROMISSO CONTRATO_CYBER DTNASCTO DTVCTO);

	retain
	CONTRATO_2
	CONTRATO_CYBER
	CPFCNPJ
	TP_PESSOA
	NOME_CLIENTE
	RISCO_1
	RISCO_2
	RISCO_3
	DT_OCORRENCIA
	DT_NASCIMENTO
	ENDER
	COMPLEMENTO
	BAIRRO
	CIDADE
	MUNICIPIO
	UF
	CEP
	DT_COMPROMISSO
	DTNASCTO
	DTVCTO;

set aux_04 ;

	format DTVCTO DTNASCTO DTCRT $8.;

	ANONASCIMENTO=PUT(YEAR(datepart(DT_NASCIMENTO)),Z4.);
	MESNASCIMENTO=PUT(MONTH(datepart(DT_NASCIMENTO)),z2.);
	DIANASCIMENTO=PUT(DAY(datepart(DT_NASCIMENTO)),z2.);

	DTNASCTO=ANONASCIMENTO||MESNASCIMENTO||DIANASCIMENTO;

	ANOCOMPRO=PUT(YEAR(DT_OCORRENCIA),Z4.);
	MESCOMPRO=PUT(MONTH(DT_OCORRENCIA),z2.);
	DIACOMPRO=PUT(DAY(DT_OCORRENCIA),z2.);

	DTVCTO=ANOCOMPRO||MESCOMPRO||DIACOMPRO;

	format ENDER $45.;

	ENDER=TRIM(COMPRESS(ENDERECO))||' '||', '||TRIM(COMPRESS(NuEndereco))||' '||TRANWRD(TRIM(COMPRESS(COMPLEMENTO)),'NULL','');

	;
;
run;



data Nometamanho (KEEP= CONTRATO_CYBER N COMPLE_NOME) ;
set base_aux_cadastro;
TAMANHONOME=LENGTH(NOME_CLIENTE);
n=0;
	do while (n<=(70-TAMANHONOME));
	COMPLE_NOME='.';
	N=N+1;
	OUTPUT;
	end;

RUN;

 
        PROC SORT
        	DATA=WORK.Nometamanho(KEEP=COMPLE_NOME CONTRATO_CYBER)
        	OUT=WORK.SORTTempTableSorted;
        	BY CONTRATO_CYBER;
        RUN;
		PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=WORK.SORTTempTableSorted
        	OUT=WORK.TRNSTransposed(LABEL="Transposed WORK.Nometamanho")
        	PREFIX=Column
        	NAME=Source
        	LABEL=Label;
        	BY CONTRATO_CYBER;
        	VAR COMPLE_NOME;
       RUN;

DATA NOME_TAMANHOFINAL (KEEP=CONTRATO_CYBER NOME_TAMANHO);
SET TRNSTRANSPOSED;
FORMAT NOME_TAMANHO $61.;
		NOME_TAMANHO=CATS(Column1,Column2,Column3,Column4,Column5,Column6,Column7,Column8,Column9,Column10,
						  Column11,Column12,Column13,Column14,Column15,Column16,Column17,Column18,Column19,Column20,
						  Column21,Column22,Column23,Column24,Column25,Column26,Column27,Column28,Column29,Column30,
						  Column31,Column32,Column33,Column34,Column35,Column36,Column37,Column38,Column39,Column40,
						  Column41,Column42,Column43,Column44,Column45,Column46,Column47,Column48,Column49,Column50,
						  Column51,Column52,Column53,Column54,Column55,Column56,Column57,Column58,Column59,Column60,
						  Column61);
RUN;



data  ENDERtamanho (KEEP= CONTRATO_CYBER N COMPLE_ENDER);
set base_aux_cadastro;
TAMANHOENDER=LENGTH(ENDER);
n=0;
	do while (n<=(45-TAMANHOENDER));

	N=N+1;

        	DATA=WORK.ENDERtamanho(KEEP=COMPLE_ENDER CONTRATO_CYBER)

		ENDER_TAMANHO=CATS(Column1,Column2,Column3,Column4,Column5,Column6,Column7,Column8,Column9,Column10,
						  Column11,Column12,Column13,Column14,Column15,Column16,Column17,Column18,Column19,Column20,

						  Column31,Column32,Column33,Column34,Column35,Column36,Column37,Column38,Column39,Column40,
						  Column41,Column42,Column43);
RUN;



data BA_tamanhoBAIRRO (KEEP= CONTRATO_CYBER N COMPLE_BAIRRO);
set  base_aux_cadastro;

TAMANHOBAIRRO=LENGTH(BAIRRO);
n=0;
	do while (n<=(20-TAMANHOBAIRRO));
	COMPLE_BAIRRO='.';
	N=N+1;
	OUTPUT;
	end;

RUN;

PROC SQL;
SELECT MAX(N) AS MAX FROM BA_tamanhoBAIRRO; RUN;

 
        PROC SORT
        	DATA=WORK.BA_tamanhoBAIRRO(KEEP=COMPLE_BAIRRO CONTRATO_CYBER)
        	OUT=WORK.SORTTempTableSorted;
        	BY CONTRATO_CYBER;
        RUN;
		PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=WORK.SORTTempTableSorted
        	OUT=WORK.TRNSTransposedBAIRRO(LABEL="Transposed WORK.COMPLE_BAIRRO")
        	PREFIX=Column
        	NAME=Source
        	LABEL=Label;
        	BY CONTRATO_CYBER;
        	VAR COMPLE_BAIRRO;
       RUN;

DATA BASE_BAIRROFINAL (KEEP=CONTRATO_CYBER BAIRRO_TAMANHO);
SET TRNSTransposedBAIRRO;
FORMAT BAIRRO_TAMANHO $20.;
		BAIRRO_TAMANHO=CATS(Column1,Column2,Column3,Column4,Column5,Column6,Column7,Column8,Column9,Column10,
						  Column11,Column12,Column13,Column14,Column15,Column16,Column17,Column18,Column19,Column20,
						  Column21,Column22,Column23,Column24,Column25,Column26,Column27,Column28,Column29,Column30,
						  Column31,Column32,Column33);
RUN;



data  BA_tamanhoMUNIC (KEEP= CONTRATO_CYBER N COMPLE_MUNIC);
set base_aux_cadastro;

MUNICIPIO=CIDADE;

TAMANHOMUNIC=LENGTH(trim(MUNICIPIO));
n=0;
	do while (n<=(25-TAMANHOMUNIC));
	COMPLE_MUNIC='.';
	N=N+1;
	OUTPUT;
	end;

RUN;

PROC SQL;
SELECT MAX(N) AS MAX FROM BA_tamanhoMUNIC; RUN;

 
        PROC SORT
        	DATA=WORK.BA_tamanhoMUNIC(KEEP=COMPLE_MUNIC CONTRATO_CYBER)
        	OUT=WORK.SORTTempTableSorted;
        	BY CONTRATO_CYBER;
        RUN;
		PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=WORK.SORTTempTableSorted
        	OUT=WORK.TRNSTransposedMUNIC(LABEL="Transposed WORK.COMPLE_MUNIC")
        	PREFIX=Column
        	NAME=Source
        	LABEL=Label;

       RUN;

DATA BASE_MUNICFINAL (KEEP=CONTRATO_CYBER MUNIC_TAMANHO);
SET TRNSTransposedMUNIC;
FORMAT MUNIC_TAMANHO $25.;
		MUNIC_TAMANHO=CATS(Column1,Column2,Column3,Column4,Column5,Column6,Column7,Column8,Column9,Column10,
						  Column11,Column12,Column13,Column14,Column15,Column16,Column17,Column18,Column19,Column20,
						  Column21,Column22,Column23,Column24,Column25,Column26,Column27,Column28,Column29,Column30,
						  Column31,Column32,Column33,Column34);
RUN;

proc sql;
create table BASE_CADASTRO_REAB_A as select
	TODAY() AS DTGERACAO FORMAT DDMMYY10.,
	a.*,
	b.NOME_TAMANHO,
	C.ENDER_TAMANHO,
	D.BAIRRO_TAMANHO,
	E.MUNIC_TAMANHO

from base_aux_cadastro a
left join NOME_TAMANHOFINAL b
on a.CONTRATO_CYBER=b.CONTRATO_CYBER
LEFT JOIN TAMANHOENDER_FINAL C
ON a.CONTRATO_CYBER=C.CONTRATO_CYBER
LEFT JOIN BASE_BAIRROFINAL D
ON a.CONTRATO_CYBER=D.CONTRATO_CYBER
LEFT JOIN BASE_MUNICFINAL E
ON a.CONTRATO_CYBER=E.CONTRATO_CYBER
;quit;

data BASE_CADASTRO_REAB;
set BASE_CADASTRO_REAB_A (where=(CPFCNPJ not in ("",'.') and UF is not missing));
	N=_N_;
	NUSQREGISTRO=N+1;

	FORMAT NUSQREGISTRO2 $7.;
	NUSQREGISTRO2=PUT(NUSQREGISTRO,Z7.);

drop
n;
run;

proc sort data=BASE_CADASTRO_REAB;
by NUSQREGISTRO;
run;

PROC SQL;
	CONNECT TO BIGQUERY AS PERM (
		project   = 'bv-sadi-dtm'
		credfile  = '/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json'
		schema    = 'bv-pdbd-prd'
		Read_Mode = Storage
		Allow_Large_Results = must
		Max_Char_Len = 40
		);
	CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_CHASSI_1 AS
		SELECT DISTINCT A.*
			FROM CONNECTION TO PERM
				(
			SELECT
NuContrato,
CdMarca,
CdModelo,
SgUfPlaca,
SgUfLicenciamento,
AaFabricacao,
AaModelo,
DtInclusao,
DsCor,
nuchassi

		FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dbgestaocdccg.tbobjgarantiabemveiculo) AS A
	;
	DISCONNECT FROM PERM
	;
QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE BASE_CHASSI_2 AS
SELECT
	A.*,
	b.QTATRASO as ATRASO_2025,
case when A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO then 1 else 0 end as flag_carteira
FROM WORK.BASE_CHASSI_1 AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.CARTEIRA_RODAS_EMP_2025	AS B ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO
;QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 114 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 115 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.FILTER_FOR_BASE_CHASSI_2);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.FILTER_FOR_BASE_CHASSI_2 AS
   SELECT t1.NuContrato,
          t1.CdMarca,
          t1.CdModelo,
          t1.SgUfPlaca,
          t1.SgUfLicenciamento,
          t1.AaFabricacao,
          t1.AaModelo,
          t1.DtInclusao,
          t1.DsCor,
          t1.nuchassi,
          t1.ATRASO_2025
      FROM WORK.BASE_CHASSI_2 t1
      WHERE t1.flag_carteira = 1;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 115 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 116 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
data CARTERIA_ATRASO;
	set CARTEIRA_RODAS_EMP(where=(QTATRASO > 5 and QTATRASO < 70 and NUCONTRATO is not null and cd_cpf_cnpj is not null));

	format pub_lembrete $35.;
	format valida_pub_1 $35.;
	format valida_pub_2 $35.;
	format valida_pub_3 $35.;

	rdg_uso1=compress(substr(NUCONTRATO,12,1) || substr(cd_cpf_cnpj, 6,1));

	rdg_uso2=compress(substr(cd_cpf_cnpj, 6,1) || substr(cd_cpf_cnpj, 7,1));

	rdg_uso3=compress(substr(cd_cpf_cnpj, 7,1) || substr(cd_cpf_cnpj, 8,1));

	rdg_proposta=compress(substr(cd_cpf_cnpj, 8,1) || substr(NUCONTRATO,12,1));

	if rdg_proposta < 49 then pub_lembrete='TESTE';
	else pub_lembrete='CONTROLE';

	if rdg_uso1 < 49 then valida_pub_1='TESTE';
	else valida_pub_1='CONTROLE';

	if rdg_uso2 < 49 then valida_pub_2='TESTE';
	else valida_pub_2='CONTROLE';

	if rdg_uso3 < 49 then valida_pub_3='TESTE';
	else valida_pub_3='CONTROLE';
run;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 116 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 117 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.SORT);

PROC SORT
	DATA=WORK.CARTERIA_ATRASO(KEEP=rdg_uso1 rdg_uso2 rdg_uso3 rdg_proposta)
	OUT=WORK.SORT
	;
	BY rdg_proposta;
RUN;

TITLE;
TITLE1 "One-Way Frequencies";
TITLE2 "Results";
FOOTNOTE;
FOOTNOTE1 "Generated by the SAS System (&_SASSERVERNAME, &SYSSCPL) on %TRIM(%QSYSFUNC(DATE(), NLDATE20.)) at %TRIM(%SYSFUNC(TIME(), TIMEAMPM12.))";
PROC FREQ DATA=WORK.SORT
	ORDER=INTERNAL
;
	TABLES rdg_uso1 /  SCORES=TABLE;
	TABLES rdg_uso2 /  SCORES=TABLE;
	TABLES rdg_uso3 /  SCORES=TABLE;
	BY rdg_proposta;
RUN;

RUN; QUIT;
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.SORT);
TITLE; FOOTNOTE;

/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 117 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 118 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.QUERY_FOR_CARTERIA_ATRASO);

PROC SQL;
   CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERY_FOR_CARTERIA_ATRASO AS
   SELECT t1.nmmodalidadeproduto,
          t1.NMGRUPOSUBPRODUTO,
          t1.rdg_uso1,
          t1.rdg_uso2,
          t1.rdg_uso3,
          t1.rdg_proposta,
          
            (COUNT(t1.NUCONTRATO)) AS COUNT_of_NUCONTRATO
      FROM WORK.CARTERIA_ATRASO t1
      GROUP BY t1.nmmodalidadeproduto,
               t1.NMGRUPOSUBPRODUTO,
               t1.rdg_uso1,
               t1.rdg_uso2,
               t1.rdg_uso3,
               t1.rdg_proposta;
QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 118 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.SORT);

PROC SORT
	DATA=WORK.CARTERIA_ATRASO(KEEP=valida_pub_1 valida_pub_2 valida_pub_3 pub_lembrete)
	OUT=WORK.SORT
	;
	BY pub_lembrete;
RUN;

TITLE;
TITLE1 "One-Way Frequencies";
TITLE2 "Results";
FOOTNOTE;
FOOTNOTE1 "Generated by the SAS System (&_SASSERVERNAME, &SYSSCPL) on %TRIM(%QSYSFUNC(DATE(), NLDATE20.)) at %TRIM(%SYSFUNC(TIME(), TIMEAMPM12.))";
PROC FREQ DATA=WORK.SORT
	ORDER=INTERNAL
;
	TABLES valida_pub_1 /  SCORES=TABLE;
	TABLES valida_pub_2 /  SCORES=TABLE;
	TABLES valida_pub_3 /  SCORES=TABLE;
	BY pub_lembrete;
RUN;

RUN; QUIT;
%_eg_conditional_dropds(WORK.SORT);
TITLE; FOOTNOTE;

/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 120 */
/* FLUXO: (embedded — project.xml) */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
proc sql;
connect to bigquery as perm(project='bv-sadi-dtm' 
credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json' 
schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage  MAX_CHAR_LEN=100);

Create table WORK.TAB_ANALISE_PGTOS as select * from connection to perm
( SELECT
a.*,
sum(SAFE_CAST(b.APAMTPAY AS float64)) AS total_pago,
CASE WHEN sum(SAFE_CAST(b.APAMTPAY AS float64)) > 0 THEN max(b.APDETID)
ELSE '0' END AS ult_parc_pg
FROM

(
SELECT * from
(SELECT
AHID, AHTYPE, AHACCTG, AHACCT, AHDT, AHCOLLID, AHAUTDT, AHAUTCOLLID, AHTOTPMT,  AHPRD, AHDESCPAMT, AHVLIOF, AHSTATUS, AHDSCQB ,AHFLTPIOF
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr
UNION ALL
SELECT
AHID, AHTYPE, AHACCTG, AHACCT, AHDT, AHCOLLID, AHAUTDT, AHAUTCOLLID, AHTOTPMT,  AHPRD, AHDESCPAMT, AHVLIOF , AHSTATUS, AHDSCQB ,AHFLTPIOF
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agrhdr_esp)
WHERE AHACCT IN ('0020003033363', '0020002754557')
) a

LEFT JOIN
( SELECT
APAHID, APDETID, APINDATE, APDUEDT,APAMT, APAMTPAY,APFLAG,APVLIOFD, APVLIOFA, APVLIOF
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet
UNION all
SELECT
APAHID, APDETID, APINDATE, APDUEDT, APAMT,APAMTPAY,APFLAG,APVLIOFD, APVLIOFA, APVLIOF
FROM bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_rcvry.agpmtdet_esp) b
ON a.AHID = b.APAHID
GROUP BY
a.AHID, a.AHTYPE, a.AHACCTG, a.AHACCT, a.AHDT, a.AHCOLLID, a.AHAUTDT, a.AHAUTCOLLID, a.AHTOTPMT,  a.AHPRD, a.AHDESCPAMT, a.AHVLIOF, a.AHSTATUS, a.AHDSCQB ,a.AHFLTPIOF
ORDER BY AHDT desc
);
disconnect from perm;
quit;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 120 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
